### [33] [MonaVec: A Training-Free Embedded Vector Search Kernel for Edge and Offline AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19458)
*Oğuzhan Yenen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MonaVec是一个确定性、无需训练的嵌入式向量搜索内核，专为边缘和离线AI设计。它通过随机哈达玛变换和预计算Lloyd-Max表实现4位量化（8倍压缩），以单个.mvec文件持久化索引，支持跨架构字节一致的可复现性。在AG News数据集上，4位暴力搜索在27MB内存中达到0.960 Recall@10，超越float32 FAISS-IVF和8位usearch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量搜索系统依赖持久化服务器、GB级内存或数据训练，无法满足边缘和离线场景（无服务器基础设施、无网络连接、无训练数据）的需求。作者希望开发一个类似SQLite的嵌入式向量搜索方案：一个文件、一次函数调用、随处运行。

Method: 1) 使用随机哈达玛变换(RHDH)将任意输入分布映射到近似N(0,1)分布；2) 利用预计算Lloyd-Max量化表进行4位训练无关量化；3) 嵌入ChaCha20旋转种子保证跨架构可复现性；4) 支持全局标准化扩展至L2数据；5) 提供IvfFlat和HNSW后端支持百万级向量。

Result: 在AG News数据集（45K x 1024维BGE-M3嵌入，余弦相似度）上，MonaVec 4位暴力搜索仅用27MB内存达到0.960 Recall@10，召回率领先float32 FAISS-IVF和8位usearch。牺牲峰值吞吐量换取字节级确定性的可复现性。

Conclusion: MonaVec填补了边缘AI和离线场景下向量搜索的空白，提供类似SQLite的部署体验：单文件、单函数调用、跨平台运行。纯Rust实现，支持Python绑定和运行时SIMD调度，面向设备端RAG、离线智能体和嵌入式检索。

Abstract: We present MonaVec, a deterministic, embedded vector-search kernel for edge and offline AI -- settings where server infrastructure, network connectivity, and training data are all unavailable. Existing vector-search systems assume a persistent server, gigabytes of RAM, or a training pass over the corpus; MonaVec instead targets the deployment profile of SQLite: one file, one function call, runs anywhere. Its quantization core is training-free by default and data-oblivious: a Randomized Hadamard Transform (RHDH) conditions any input distribution toward N(0,1), so precomputed Lloyd-Max tables quantize to 4 bits (8x smaller) with no learned codebook and no data pass. The index persists as a single .mvec file whose embedded ChaCha20 rotation seed makes results reproducible across architectures and byte-identical within a build -- a determinism guarantee that parallel-build graph libraries cannot offer.
  On semantic embeddings (AG News, 45K x 1024-dim BGE-M3, cosine), MonaVec 4-bit BruteForce reaches 0.960 Recall@10 in 27 MB -- leading float32 FAISS-IVF and 8-bit usearch on recall -- while trading peak throughput for byte-identical determinism. A single-pass global standardization (fit()) extends the same data-oblivious pipeline to magnitude-sensitive L2 data, and optional IvfFlat and HNSW backends carry it to million-vector corpora.
  MonaVec is implemented in pure Rust with Python bindings and runtime SIMD dispatch (AVX-512/AVX2/NEON/scalar). It targets on-device RAG, offline agents, and embedded retrieval -- the niche SQLite occupies for relational data: one file, one call, runs anywhere.

</details>


### [34] [VCG: A Multimodal Retrieval Framework for E-Commerce Video Feeds under Extreme Cold-Start Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19627)
*Katya Mirylenka,Egor Malykh,Mahdyar Ravanbakhsh,Michael Gygli,Marco-Andrea Buchmann,Andrew Dzhoha,Svitlana Borzenko,Francesca Catino,Mohamed Gaafar,Maarten Versteegh,Thomas Kober,Dario d'Andrea,Ellie Langhans*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了VCG系统，一种基于视觉-语言模型（CLIP）的多模态检索引擎，用于解决短视频电商中极端冷启动、位置偏差和时长偏差问题。在线A/B测试显示，VCG将深度视频完成率提升了50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字商务正从静态搜索型目录转向动态沉浸式视频流，但新上架的短视频缺乏协同过滤所需的密集交互历史（极端冷启动问题），且沉浸式feed存在强烈的位置和时长偏差，扭曲了标准参与信号。

Method: 构建VCG系统：采用领域适配的CLIP视觉-语言模型，将用户和视频映射到共享语义空间，实现基于视觉内容的零样本检索。系统支持三种双向检索场景：Product-to-Video、Video-to-Product和Zero-Shot Semantic Search。

Result: 在线A/B测试表明VCG有效缓解了参与偏差，深度视频完成率提升50%。对比实验发现，生成式模型（LLM）在属性预测上表现优异，但在检索任务中存在嵌入空间坍塌问题。

Conclusion: VCG通过视觉语义对齐解决了短视频电商的冷启动和偏差问题，且判别式嵌入（CLIP）在检索任务中优于生成式嵌入（LLM）。

Abstract: The digital commerce landscape is shifting from static, search-driven catalogs to dynamic, immersive video feeds. This transition introduces an ``extreme cold-start'' problem: unlike traditional items, new short-form videos lack the dense interaction history required for collaborative filtering. Furthermore, immersive feeds introduce strong position and duration biases that distort standard engagement signals. In this paper, we demonstrate the Video Candidate Generation (VCG) system, a scalable multimodal retrieval engine designed to solve these challenges in a large-scale e-commerce environment. By leveraging a domain-adapted vision-language model (based on CLIP), we map users and videos into a shared semantic space, enabling zero-shot retrieval based on visual content rather than behavioral history. We detail the system's architecture and present a rigorous evaluation comparing generative (LLM) vs. discriminative (CLIP) embeddings. Our results show that while generative models excel at attribute prediction, they suffer from embedding space collapse in retrieval tasks. Online A/B testing demonstrates that VCG effectively mitigates engagement biases, yielding a 50\% uplift in deep video completion. To showcase the system's capabilities, we present an interactive demonstration featuring three bi-directional retrieval scenarios: Product-to-Video, Video-to-Product, and Zero-Shot Semantic Search.

</details>


### [35] [Token Factory: Efficiently Integrating Diverse Signals into Large Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19635)
*Xilun Chen,Shao-Chuan Wang,Baykal Cakici,Lukasz Heldt,Lichan Hong,Raghu Keshavan,Aniruddh Nath,Li Wei,Xinyang Xi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Token Factory 是一个将传统推荐信号转化为“软令牌”（soft tokens）以高效集成到大型推荐模型（LRMs）中的框架，解决了传统方法中提示过长、内存占用大和计算开销高的问题，并在生产级推荐系统中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推荐模型在工业级推荐任务中表现出色，但如何高效且有效地将传统信号（如用户行为、上下文特征等）集成到基于Transformer的架构中仍是一个重大挑战。现有方法（如将信号直接“文本化”或创建离散物品表示）会导致提示过长、内存占用大和计算开销高。

Method: 提出Token Factory框架，将传统信号转换为“软令牌”（soft tokens），使其能够直接被大型推荐模型处理。该方法实现了异构输入特征的高效集成与压缩，防止提示长度爆炸的同时提升模型性能。

Result: 在生产级推荐环境中进行的实验验证了Token Factory的有效性，表明该方法能够有效集成传统信号并提升推荐性能。

Conclusion: Token Factory通过将传统信号转化为软令牌，成功解决了LRM中异构特征集成时的效率与效果权衡问题，为工业级推荐系统提供了一种可行的优化方案。

Abstract: Large Recommendation Models (LRMs) have demonstrated promising capabilities in industry-scale recommendation tasks. However, holistically integrating traditional signals into these transformer-based architectures effectively and efficiently remains a major challenge. Conventional approaches that "textualize" these signals directly or create discrete item representations often lead to excessively long prompts, substantial memory footprints, and high computational overhead. To overcome these limitations, we propose "Token Factory", a framework designed to transform traditional signals into "soft tokens" that can be directly processed by LRMs. This approach enables efficient integration and compression of heterogeneous input features, preventing prompt length explosion while enhancing model performance. We detail the architecture of Token Factory and present experimental results validating its effectiveness in a production-scale recommendation environment.

</details>


### [36] [SAFE-Cascade: Cost-Adaptive Vision-Language Routing for Chart Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19646)
*Ayush Dwivedi,Qixin Wang,Ashvi Soni,Ruoteng Wang,Han Li,Animesh Mahapatra,Neeraj Agrawal,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAFE-Cascade是一个面向图表问答的成本自适应系统，通过OCR提取文本后先用纯文本语言模型回答，再由学习路由器决定是否升级到VLM，在保持与全VLM基线相当准确率（69.1% vs 67.7%）的同时，减少了26.9%的VLM调用和9.3%的估计成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每次查询都调用VLM成本高昂，而许多图表问题仅通过OCR文本和轻量级语言推理即可回答，因此需要一种成本自适应的选择性路由机制来降低推理成本。

Method: 系统由OCR提取、纯文本语言模型推理、基于随机森林的路由器（以推理时特征为输入）三部分组成。路由器决定是接受文本答案还是升级到VLM，并允许用户调整阈值探索精度-成本权衡。

Result: 在375个样本的ChartQA测试集上，SAFE-Cascade达到69.1%的统一准确率（全VLM基线为67.7%），VLM调用率降至73.1%，估计成本降低9.3%。

Conclusion: 选择性模态路由可以使多模态知识系统更加透明、可调优和成本感知，在保持性能的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful for chart question answering, but invoking a VLM for every query can be unnecessarily expensive when many questions are answerable from OCR text and lightweight language reasoning. We demonstrate SAFE-Cascade, an interactive system for cost-adaptive chart question answering. Given a chart image and a natural-language question, SAFE-Cascade first extracts chart text with OCR, obtains a provisional answer from a text-only language model, and then uses a learned router to decide whether to accept the text answer or escalate to a VLM. The demo exposes this decision process to users: OCR evidence, text-only answer, routing probability, escalation decision, final answer, estimated cost, and estimated latency are shown side by side. SAFE-Cascade is designed as a transparent interface for understanding when visual grounding is actually needed. Users can upload or select charts, ask questions, inspect the evidence used by each pathway, compare text-only and VLM answers, and adjust the escalation threshold to explore the accuracy-cost frontier. The system is implemented with Azure Document Intelligence for OCR, gpt-5-mini as the text-only model, gemini-2.5-flash-image as the VLM, and a Random Forest router trained on inference-time features. On a held-out ChartQA test split of 375 examples from a 2,500-example experiment, SAFE-Cascade achieves 69.1% unified accuracy with 73.1% VLM invocation, compared with 67.7% accuracy and 100% VLM invocation for the full-VLM baseline. The observed +1.4 percentage-point difference is statistically uncertain, so we interpret SAFE-Cascade as matching full-VLM performance while reducing VLM calls by 26.9% and estimated cost by 9.3%. The demonstration shows how selective modality routing can make multimodal knowledge systems more transparent, tunable, and cost-aware.

</details>


### [37] [Closing the Calibration Gap in Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19719)
*Aditeya Baral,Radoslav Ralev,Iliya Sotirov Zhechev,Srijith Rajamohan,Jen Agarwal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出现有语义缓存系统用PR-AUC评估性能会导致部署决策失误，提出P-CHR AUC和CRR两个新指标，证明模型选择本质是校准问题而非排序问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义缓存系统使用PR-AUC作为评估指标，但该指标仅衡量排序质量，忽略了固定阈值下的可用性，导致离线表现最优的模型在实际部署中效果最差。

Method: 提出P-CHR AUC（衡量缓存利用率各层级上的精度）和CRR（量化离线排序质量在部署中的留存程度）；将离线与部署质量差距分解为可恢复的校准分量和由数据集正例率决定的不可约结构分量。

Result: 校准差距由训练目标决定而非数据规模；事后校准只能部分弥合该差距；PR-AUC最优的模型在部署中表现最差。

Conclusion: 语义缓存的模型选择是校准问题而非排序问题，正确度量是弥合离线与部署性能差距的第一步。

Abstract: Semantic caching cuts LLM inference costs by serving a cached response to semantically similar queries. Standard practice evaluates these systems using PR-AUC, a metric that only measures how well scores rank and ignores whether they are usable at a fixed threshold. We show this mismatch leads to systematically poor deployment choices, as models with the highest PR-AUC are often the worst in operation. We introduce Precision-Cache Hit Ratio (P-CHR) AUC, a cache-aware metric that measures precision across cache utilization levels, and Calibration Retention Rate (CRR), which captures how much offline ranking quality survives at deployment. We decompose the operational gap between offline and deployed quality into a recoverable calibration component and an irreducible structural component fixed by the dataset's positive rate. Our experiments show that the calibration gap is governed by the training objective rather than data scale, and post-hoc calibration only partially closes it. Ultimately, model selection for semantic caching is a calibration problem, not a ranking one, and measuring it is the first step to closing the gap.

</details>


### [38] [Stellar: Scalable Multimodal Document Retrieval for Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19960)
*Yuxiang Guo,Zhonghao Hu,Yuren Mao,Yuhang Liu,Congcong Ge,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Stellar提出了一种可扩展的多模态文档检索框架，通过基于词汇表示的高效过滤和磁盘支持的延迟交互机制，在不牺牲检索效果的前提下，将内存开销和查询延迟降低1-2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多模态文档检索方法使用多向量表示和延迟交互虽然效果优异，但token级嵌入导致巨大的内存开销，可扩展性差，难以实际部署。

Method: Stellar包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于词汇表示的过滤（LRF）：微调多模态大语言模型作为稀疏编码器，生成高质量词汇表示以高效过滤候选文档；(2) 高效磁盘支持的延迟交互（DLI）：通过平衡聚类算法指导磁盘嵌入存储布局，并利用成本模型动态加载必要嵌入到内存。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准和一个新的大规模数据集上，Stellar将内存开销和查询延迟降低了1-2个数量级，同时不损失检索效果。

Conclusion: Stellar通过将文档嵌入存储到磁盘并有选择地加载到内存，有效解决了多向量检索的可扩展性问题，为多模态RAG系统的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal document retrieval--selecting the most relevant multimodal document from a large corpus to answer a natural language query--plays an essential role in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems. State-of-the-art methods represent each document and query with multiple token-level embeddings and use late interaction to achieve high effectiveness. However, such multi-vector representations incur substantial memory overhead during retrieval, leading to poor scalability and hindering real-world deployment. In this paper, we present Stellar, a scalable multimodal document retrieval framework that stores token-level document embeddings on disk and loads only a small set of candidate embeddings into memory for late interaction. Stellar comprises two key components: (i) Lexical Representation-based Filtering (LRF), which fine-tunes a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a sparse encoder to produce high-quality lexical representations, enabling efficient and effective document filtering to significantly reduce the candidate set; (ii) Efficient Disk-backed Late Interaction (DLI), which designs an on-disk token embedding storage layout guided by a balanced clustering algorithm, and dynamically loads only the necessary token embeddings into memory using a simple yet effective cost model. Extensive experiments on four real-world benchmarks and a newly presented large-scale dataset demonstrate that Stellar reduces memory overhead and query latency by 1-2 orders of magnitude compared to existing methods without compromising retrieval effectiveness.

</details>


### [39] [PACMS: Submodular Context Selection as a Pluggable Engine for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20047)
*Manu Ghulyani,Arunabh Singh,Karan Bharadwaj,Ankit Nath,Suranjan Goswami*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 当前LLM智能体上下文窗口被对话记录、持久记忆和工具调用输出多方向填充，现有截断机制存在主题盲区，而检索增强和上下文压缩方法均未能将已有上下文作为统一候选池进行相关选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有截断机制（如近因截断）对主题无感知，丢弃早期重要信息而保留近期无关内容，导致需要跨轮次记忆的智能体场景下性能严重下降。

Method: 将记忆条目、对话轮次、工具输出视为统一候选池，在提示组装时按相关性进行选择，而非基于位置或压缩。

Result: 提出一种新的上下文选择范式，能够在提示构建时刻根据当前查询的相关性来动态选择保留哪些上下文内容。

Conclusion: 现有方法（近因截断、RAG、上下文压缩）均未能解决从智能体已有上下文中按相关性选择的问题，需要新的机制来统一管理多源上下文。

Abstract: Conversational and tool-using LLM agents operate over a context window that fills from several directions simultaneously. As a session proceeds, the agent accumulates user and assistant turns, entries drawn from a persistent memory store, and often largest of all, the verbatim outputs of tool calls such as file reads, search results, and API responses. Once the cumulative context exceeds the model's token budget, the framework must decide what to keep.
  The prevailing mechanism is recency truncation, sometimes paired with periodic summarization. This is topic-blind: a fact established early in a session is discarded simply because it is old, even when the current user query is about exactly that fact; conversely, verbose but irrelevant recent material is retained. Agents that must recall information across many turns, the defining case for memory, are precisely where recency truncation fails.
  Existing alternatives sit outside the agent's assembly step. Retrieval augmented generation fetches external documents into the prompt but does not arbitrate the agent's \emph{already-present} pooled context. Context-compression methods reduce token count by rewriting or pruning text, but operate query-blind and lossily. Neither treats memory entries, conversation turns, and tool outputs as a single candidate pool to be selected from by relevance at the moment the prompt is assembled.

</details>


### [40] [Generative Engine Optimization at Scale: Measuring Brand Visibility Across AI Search Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20065)
*Pratyush Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式引擎优化（GEO）下品牌在AI搜索引擎中的可见性。通过分析10万+提示词响应和100+品牌数据，发现品牌可见性呈现三级阶梯结构（全球知名品牌73%、中型品牌44%、小众品牌11%），78%的引用来自企业官网，"最佳"列表类内容是最常被引用的格式（21%），情感倾向的波动性（6.7倍）远高于提及率波动。研究最后提出七个v1.1协议用于测试GEO策略的因果效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户越来越多地从ChatGPT、Claude、Perplexity、Gemini等AI搜索引擎直接获取答案，传统SEO已无法满足品牌在AI时代的可见性需求。特别是对于非顶级品牌的中小企业、DTC品牌、创作者和早期初创企业而言，缺乏关于AI引擎如何评估、引用和推荐品牌的大规模基线数据，因此需要系统性地研究GEO的测量方法和品牌可见性规律。

Method: 收集2026年3月至5月期间Ranqo平台上100+品牌的10万+提示词响应数据，分析品牌在AI答案中的出现频率、引用来源分布、内容格式偏好以及情感倾向的稳定性。研究将AEO和AI可见性纳入GEO框架，建立品牌成熟度与可见性的关联模型。

Result: 品牌可见性呈现明确的三级阶梯：全球知名品牌在首次运行时出现在73%的相关AI回答中，中型区域品牌为44%，小众品牌仅11%，每级差距约30个百分点。引用来源中78%为企业官网，非企业来源中YouTube领先于Reddit、编辑媒体和维基百科。列表类"最佳"内容是最高杠杆内容格式，占所有引用的21%。情感倾向的波动性（正面/负面框架转换频率）是提及率波动的6.7倍，极不稳定。

Conclusion: AI品牌可见性可以被测量，因平台而异，且与品牌成熟度高度相关。研究为GEO提供了首个大规模基线数据，并提出了七个v1.1协议用于验证特定推荐策略能否因果性地提升AI可见性。

Abstract: People increasingly get answers straight from AI search engines like ChatGPT, Claude, Perplexity, and Gemini rather than scrolling search results. Brands that once focused on search engine optimization (SEO) must now optimize for how these engines represent, cite, and recommend them -- a shift variously called Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), Answer Engine Optimization (AEO), and AI Search Visibility. We treat AEO and AI Visibility as part of GEO, and study how to measure brand visibility across AI engines: what they value when they cite a brand, which sources they rely on, and what content large language models surface. The hard case is everyone outside the already-authoritative top brands -- SMEs, D2C brands, creators, and early-stage startups.
  We analyze 100K+ prompt responses across 100+ brands tracked on Ranqo between March and May 2026. First visibility runs form a clear three-tier brand-stature ladder: global household names (e.g., Stripe, Nike) appear in 73% of relevant AI answers on their first run; established mid-market and regional brands (e.g., Olipop, Klaviyo) in 44%; niche and small brands in just 11% -- about 30 percentage points per step. When engines cite sources, about 78% go to corporate websites; among non-corporate sources YouTube leads, ahead of Reddit, editorial media, and Wikipedia. The highest-leverage page is the ranked "best-of" listicle, the most-cited content format at about 21% of all citations. Sentiment is the unstable signal: whether a brand is framed positively or negatively flips about 6.7 times more often than whether it is mentioned at all.
  These findings provide a first large-scale baseline for measuring GEO: AI brand visibility can be measured, differs by platform, and varies strongly by brand maturity. We close by proposing seven v1.1 protocols to test whether specific recommendations can causally improve AI visibility.

</details>


### [41] [ScholarQuest: A Taxonomy-Guided Benchmark for Agentic Academic Paper Search in Open Literature Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20235)
*Tingyue Pan,Mingyue Cheng,Daoyu Wang,Yitong Zhou,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了ScholarQuest，一个大规模、基于分类法的agentic学术论文搜索基准，包含1000多个CS主题和四种研究意图，实验表明现有最优agent方法仅达到0.314 Recall@100和0.355 Recall@All，仍有巨大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准不足以在真实开放文献环境下系统评估基于LLM的agentic学术搜索，亟需一个可扩展、可复现的评估框架。

Method: 从1000多个计算机科学主题和四种代表性研究意图（方法导向、设定锚定、比较型、范围控制）构建ScholarQuest，提供可扩展的答案构建和共享检索后端ScholarBase。

Result: agentic方法优于单次检索基线，但最佳agent仅达到0.314 Recall@100和0.355 Recall@All；进一步分析了搜索效率、意图层面的鲁棒性和失败案例。

Conclusion: ScholarQuest能够提供多维评估信号，揭示当前agentic学术搜索方法的不足，指明该领域仍有巨大的改进空间。

Abstract: Academic paper search is a core step in scientific research, and LLM-based search agents are emerging as a promising paradigm for iterative, intent-driven literature exploration. However, existing benchmarks are insufficient for systematically evaluating agentic academic search under realistic open literature environments. We propose ScholarQuest, a large-scale, taxonomy-guided benchmark for agentic academic paper search. ScholarQuest is constructed from over 1,000 computer science topics and four representative research intents, including method-oriented, setting-anchored, comparison-based, and scope-controlled queries. It further provides scalable answer construction and a shared retrieval backend ScholarBase for reproducible evaluation. Benchmarking results show that agentic methods outperform single-shot retrieval baselines, yet the best-performing agent only achieves 0.314 Recall@100 and 0.355 Recall@All, indicating substantial room for improvement. In addition, analyses of search efficiency, intent-level robustness, and failure cases further highlight the benchmark's ability to provide multi-dimensional evaluation signals for academic paper search agents.

</details>


### [42] [ELVA: Exploring Ranking-Driven Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20280)
*Yuhan Liu,Pei Fu,Hang Li,Yukun Qi,Chao Jiang,Jingwen Fu,Zhen Liu,Bin Qin,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Jingmin Xin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ELVA框架，通过基于规则的强化学习（RLVR）缓解多模态大语言模型在通用多模态检索中的“粒度盲视”问题，即模型忽视查询中粒度信息的倾向。ELVA无需奖励模型，通过排序驱动优化负样本排序并扩大正负样本相似度差距。同时引入MRBench基准评估多粒度查询性能。ELVA在标准检索基准上取得SOTA，在MRBench上提升13.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对比学习的多模态检索方法存在“粒度盲视”（grain blindness）问题，即模型忽视查询中的粒度级别信息，因为对比学习将样本视为二分类（正/负），忽略了每个负样本携带的不同信息，导致模型无法有效处理复杂多粒度查询。

Method: 提出ELVA框架：1）将具有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）拓展至检索任务，无需奖励模型，让模型探索新的排序行为；2）利用基于规则的奖励，联合优化负样本排序同时扩大正负样本相似度差距。此外，构建MRBench基准用于评估多粒度查询场景下的粒度盲视程度。

Result: ELVA在标准检索基准上达到SOTA，在MRBench上取得13.1%的提升，有效缓解了粒度盲视问题。

Conclusion: 通过将负样本按其与正样本的相似度区别对待，并引入基于规则的强化学习框架，可以有效缓解多模态检索中的粒度盲视问题，提升模型对复杂多粒度查询的处理能力。

Abstract: Leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) via contrastive learning has become a mainstream paradigm for improving the performance of Universal Multimodal Retrieval (UMR). However, previous works have ignored the grain blindness when adapting the contrastive paradigm into retrieval tasks. Grain blindness refers to the tendency of the model to overlook grain-level information contained in the query, which is crucial for effectively handling complex queries. This stems from contrastive learning treating samples as a binary classification (positive/negative), while ignoring the different information carried by each negative sample. To address this, we argue that negatives should be treated differently according to their similarity to the positive sample, enabling the model to learn distinct grain information from each negative. In this paper, we introduce a simple but effective framework, called ELVA, a novel rule-based RL framework that mitigates grain blindness through ranking-driven MLLMs. 1) Instead of relying on reward models, we extend Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards
  (RLVR) to retrieval tasks, allowing the model to explore new ranking behaviors without explicit ranking labels. 2) By utilizing rule-based rewards, our approach jointly optimizes the ranking of negative samples while enlarging the similarity gap between positive and negative. To more precisely measure grain blindness, we further introduce MRBench, a new benchmark specifically designed for multi-grain query scenarios. ELVA achieves state-of-the-art results across standard retrieval benchmarks, and its notable 13.1% improvement on MRBench further demonstrates its effectiveness in alleviating grain blindness.

</details>


### [43] [Structuring and Tokenizing Distributed User Interest Context for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20554)
*Ruizhong Qiu,Yinglong Xia,Dongqi Fu,Hanqing Zeng,Ren Chen,Xiangjun Fan,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出G2Rec框架，统一了基于图的全局长程用户协同参与建模与语义分词，用于工业规模生成式推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法在组织用户行为与物品语义上下文方面存在不足：图方法面临可扩展性问题或仅利用局部信息，语义分词方法缺乏显式监督信号导致表示不准确。

Method: 提出G2Rec框架，将全图级别的用户协同参与建模与语义分词统一，无需真实用户兴趣标签即可捕获整体且语义有依据的用户兴趣原型。

Result: 在线部署于多个产品表面，并在公开数据集上的大量实验表明G2Rec优于现有方法。

Conclusion: G2Rec能够为工业级序列推荐提供更全面、准确的用户行为上下文建模。

Abstract: Generative recommendation is an emerging paradigm that has shown promise in industrial recommendation systems, aiming to predict users' next interactions from their historical behaviors. At the core of generative recommendation lies item tokenization, which bridges item semantics and recommendation models. However, existing methods often struggle to effectively organize and inject complex user-behavioral and item-semantic contexts into recommendation models simultaneously. On the one hand, existing graph-based integration methods, such as graph serialization and graph neural networks, either suffer from scalability issues or exploit only local graph information. On the other hand, existing semantic tokenization methods typically rely on heuristics and lack explicit supervision signals, which may lead to inaccurate or suboptimal semantic representations. To address these limitations in user interest context modeling, we propose G2Rec, a scalable framework that unifies holistic graph-based user co-engagement modeling with semantic tokenization for industrial-scale generative recommendation. Overall, G2Rec enables recommendation models to capture holistic and semantically grounded user interest prototypes without requiring ground-truth user interests, thereby providing more comprehensive and accurate modeling of user behavior contexts in industrial sequential recommendation. Online deployment across product surfaces and extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superiority of G2Rec over existing methods.

</details>

### [44] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"计算可识别性"框架，区别于传统理论可识别性，通过有限计算搜索过程寻找经验估计量，在指定误差容限内满足可识别性条件，适用于小样本、模糊图标准则、混合观测-干预数据及反事实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论可识别性依赖渐近性质、无限数据等理想化数学条件，难以处理实际场景中的有限样本、模糊图标准则等问题，需要更实用的可识别性定义。

Method: 提出计算可识别性框架：定义有限计算搜索过程寻找经验估计量，若在指定误差容限内找到估计量则判定可识别，该过程依赖参数先验分布和搜索过程本身的假设。

Result: 通过实验展示了该框架能回答细粒度实用识别问题，包括小样本识别、模糊图标准则、混合观测-干预数据以及反事实数据和估计量场景。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性为实际因果推断提供了更灵活的识别判定方法，弥补了理论可识别性在有限数据、有限计算资源条件下的不足。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [45] [Learning universal approximations for partial differential equations with Physics-Informed Broad Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19754)
*Zhiwen Yu,Derong Yang,Liujian Zhang,Kaixiang Yang,Peilin Zhan,Jianmin Lv,Jane You,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息宽度学习系统（PIBLS），一种免反向传播的偏微分方程求解框架，通过直接最小二乘优化替代传统PINN，在线性和非线性PDE上实现比PINN快1-3个数量级且精度更高的求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器计算成本高（依赖网格），而PINN虽无网格但收敛慢、优化不稳定。需要一种兼具高效与精度的PDE求解方法。

Method: 提出PIBLS框架，将PDE求解转化为直接最小二乘优化（免反向传播）；改进了处理非线性PDE的算法；给出通用逼近性质的数学证明。

Result: 在线性和非线性PDE实验上，PIBLS比传统PINN快1-3个数量级，同时求解精度显著更高。

Conclusion: PIBLS为科学机器学习提供了一种计算高效的范式，是实时仿真和设计优化的实用高速替代方案。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) play a central role in modeling complex physical, biological, and engineering systems. While traditional numerical solvers are robust, they often incur prohibitive computational costs due to mesh dependencies, whereas recent Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) offer a mesh-free alternative but frequently suffer from slow convergence and optimization instability. To bridge this gap, this article proposes the Physics-Informed Broad Learning System (PIBLS), a novel backpropagation-free framework that reformulates PDE solving as a direct least-squares optimization. We improved an algorithm within this framework to handle nonlinear PDEs efficiently and provide a rigorous mathematical proof establishing the universal approximation property of PIBLS for these equations. Experiments on linear and nonlinear PDEs demonstrate that PIBLS is one to three orders of magnitude faster than conventional PINNs while achieving significantly higher solution accuracy. This framework provides a computationally efficient paradigm for scientific machine learning, offering a practical, high-speed alternative for real-time simulation and design optimization tasks.

</details>


### [46] [Evolutionary Two-Stage Hyperparameter Optimization Strategies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20442)
*Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Egor Bugaev,Andrei Ermakov,Denis Derkach,Daria Pugacheva,Fedor Ratnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段进化算法方法，通过低保真预筛选和高保真训练相结合，有效解决了PINNs在超参数优化中的不稳定性问题，在三个经典PDE问题上取得了更低的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在训练中存在收敛不稳定、训练平台期以及对架构和优化超参数高度敏感的问题，其物理信息损失函数的高度非凸和多目标特性导致外环超参数搜索成为噪声大、黑箱式的优化问题，传统局部或基于梯度的策略容易陷入次优区域。

Method: 提出两阶段进化算法方法：第一阶段进行低保真度训练（截断epoch），将超参数选择视为黑箱外环问题，利用进化算法的种群探索能力快速筛选候选配置；第二阶段仅对最有希望的候选配置使用标准梯度优化器进行完整训练以精化解。

Result: 在Advection、Klein-Gordon和Helmholtz三个经典方程上的实验表明，该方法在固定计算预算内持续优于标准训练，显著降低了平均误差。

Conclusion: 进化算法因其基于种群的探索能力和处理混合、不可微搜索空间的能力，为PINNs的超参数优化提供了更鲁棒的机制；两阶段策略在计算预算有限的情况下能有效提升求解精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into neural network training. However, their performance suffers from unstable convergence, training plateaus, and strong sensitivity to architectural and optimization hyperparameters due to the highly non-convex and multi-term structure of the physics-informed loss. In this setting, the outer-loop hyperparameter search is a noisy and black-box optimization problem over heterogeneous parameters, where classical local or gradient-based strategies are easily trapped in suboptimal regions. Evolutionary algorithms, with their population-based exploration and ability to handle mixed, non-differentiable search spaces, provide a more robust mechanism for discovering promising configurations. We propose and investigate a two-stage approach based on evolutionary algorithms that combines exploration and exploitation parts of PINNs training to improve solution accuracy and robustness under fixed computational budgets. In the first stage, we perform low-fidelity training runs with truncated epochs to rapidly screen candidate configurations, treating hyperparameter selection as a black-box outer-loop problem. In the second stage, only the most promising candidates are fully trained with standard gradient-based optimizers to refine the solution. Evaluated on three popular problems, namely Advection, Klein-Gordon and Helmholtz equations, our method consistently outperforms standard training and achieves significantly lower mean error within constrained computational resources.

</details>


### [47] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS)框架，让智能体通过进化搜索和梯度优化自动发现PDE的符号解析解或近似解，在多个问题上产生了之前未知的闭式描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家理解PDE解依赖解析结构而非数值表，但传统方法需要人工分析每个问题，数值模拟和神经网络也无法直接产生这些结构。

Method: ASYS框架：智能体将PDE理论、问题约束和搜索经验转化为可测试的符号程序，通过进化搜索优化数学形式，同时用梯度下降拟合连续参数，实现自动化归纳偏置注入。

Result: 在五个问题（有界动力学、有限时间爆破、自由边界聚焦）上产生可解释表示，包括Allen-Cahn 2D几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破九参数收缩律，这些之前没有闭式描述。

Conclusion: ASYS展示了超越手工解析解、网格数值解和神经网络近似的新范式可能性，能自动发现PDE解的符号表征。

Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

</details>


### [48] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GUARD框架，通过上下文路由器和不确定性门控温度机制，将多个分布偏移的时序基础模型的知识蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，在四个气候关键领域（气象、碳通量、土壤湿度、能源网格）实现高精度且适合边缘部署的科学预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序基础模型在物理科学中存在分布偏移导致零样本性能差，且计算成本过高无法部署于边缘传感器网络，亟需一种能从分布偏移的大模型中提取知识训练轻量级专用预测器的方法。

Method: 提出GUARD框架：1）上下文路由器，基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关的教师模型；2）不确定性门控温度机制，当教师置信度偏离领域现实时自动减弱蒸馏强度，实现实例级的多教师蒸馏决策过程。

Result: 在气象、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源电网四个领域，相比固定权重多教师蒸馏基线显著降低RMSE；证明了分布偏移的教师模型在28.5%的最难实例上仍优于全局最优的FM，可作为关键校正信号。

Conclusion: GUARD成功将分布偏移的预训练基础模型知识蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，实现了适合资源受限边缘部署的高精度科学预测。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [49] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏）方法，在边缘设备上使用4位量化小语言模型压缩用户提示词后再传输到云端大语言模型，在保留回复质量的前提下减少输入token数量，实现每调用70-270微瓦时的节能效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理的prefill阶段能耗日益增长，而用户提示词中常包含社交修饰性语言（礼貌标记、道歉性开场白、重复、建立关系语言等），这些对人类交流重要但对机器推理信息量低，存在"社交-语义鸿沟"。因此需要一种方法在保留语义和情感的前提下压缩提示词以降低能耗。

Method: 提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏）边缘管道，使用4位量化的小语言模型（Gemma-2-2B-Instruct Q4_K_M）在用户设备端压缩提示词，再传输到云端大语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）。安全关键领域通过基于规则的网关保守地路由到直通模式。

Result: 在248条提示词语料上评估：每次蒸馏调用平均节省99.9个输入token，146次蒸馏调用均实现正向节省。盲审LLM裁判评分（121对）显示蒸馏路径非劣于原始路径（预设15分制1分非劣效边界）：43%平局、28%蒸馏胜出、29%原始胜出。余弦相似度均值0.682，中位数0.712，54.1%超过0.70参考阈值。每次调用净节能估计为70-270微瓦时。

Conclusion: SPSD表明，设备端提示词蒸馏可以在实际非劣效边界内减少云端LLM输入token成本，同时保持回复质量，为降低LLM推理能耗提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>

*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

TL;DR: 本文对Med-DDPM医学扩散模型在三种NVIDIA架构上进行全面的内核级性能分析，发现训练主要由cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导，存在内存访问模式、张量布局转换和Tensor Core利用率低等低效问题。基于洞察提出的TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局优化，在A100上实现SM周期和动态指令减少高达100倍，Tensor Core利用率提升1.45-9.98倍，IPC提升7%，且不降低合成质量。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真3D MRI合成中扩散模型部署受限于大量GPU资源需求（每样本数百次U-Net评估及高度异构的内核行为），现有研究缺乏对医学扩散模型在内核级别的系统性性能分析，亟需深入理解性能瓶颈以指导优化。

Method: 在三种NVIDIA架构（Volta、Ampere、Hopper）上对Med-DDPM进行内核级运行时分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、warp级活动和profiler优先级分数估计的综合性能分析；基于发现的瓶颈，实施TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局两项架构感知优化。

Result: 训练主要由cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导；优化后SM周期减少高达100倍，动态指令减少100倍，Tensor Core利用率从1.45倍提升至9.98倍，IPC提升7%（A100），合成质量无损。

Conclusion: 架构感知的优化（TF32 Tensor Core和3D channels-last布局）能显著缓解医学扩散模型的内存访问和计算低效问题，在不牺牲合成质量的前提下大幅提升GPU资源效率，为大规模医疗影像合成部署提供了可行的优化方向。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [51] [Cost-Optimal LLM Routing with Limited User Feedback under User Satisfaction Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19376)
*Herbert Woisetschläger,Arastun Mammadli,Ryan Zhang,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLARouter是一种在线路由算法，利用生产系统中稀疏、单侧的用户反馈，学习成本最优策略，在提供严格SLA合规保证的同时，将运营成本降低高达2.2倍，且无需针对每个工作负载进行调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用推理成本快速增长，而用户对响应质量的高要求与SLA约束形成了成本与质量之间的根本性矛盾。现有成本感知路由方法依赖完整反馈信号、离线训练和大量逐工作负载调优，且多数缺乏SLA保证和推理时自适应性。

Method: 提出SLARouter，一种在线路由算法，利用生产系统中可获取的稀疏、单侧用户反馈，学习成本最优策略，并提供成本最优性和严格SLA合规的理论保证。

Result: 在多种LLM基准测试中，SLARouter满足SLA约束且无需逐基准调优，相比现有基线将运营成本降低高达2.2倍。

Conclusion: SLARouter有效解决了LLM推理中成本与质量之间的权衡，通过在线学习和理论保证实现了成本最优与SLA合规的兼顾。

Abstract: Inference costs for large language model (LLM) applications are rapidly growing, driven by surging demand and rising infrastructure cost. Users expect high-quality responses, and in commercial settings this is formally codified in Service Level Agreements (SLAs), creating a fundamental tension between cost and quality. Recent progress on cost-aware LLM request routing has shown potential to resolve this tension, but existing approaches rely on complete feedback signals, offline training, extensive per-workload tuning, and most lack SLA guarantees or inference-time adaptivity. We introduce SLARouter, an online routing algorithm that learns a cost-optimal policy from the sparse, one-sided user feedback available in production systems. SLARouter provides theoretical guarantees for both cost optimality and strict SLA compliance. Experiments across a wide range of LLM benchmarks show that SLARouter satisfies SLA constraints without the need for per-benchmark tuning, reducing operating cost by up to 2.2x over existing baselines.

</details>


### [52] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了信息格学习（ILL）的概率图模型（PGM）解释：ILL通过分区诱导确定性商变量，学习到的规则集是商变量的边际约束，一般提升等价于满足这些约束的所有联合分布族，而特殊提升（L2均匀性/最大熵）则选择最大无知重构。在Shannon熵提升下，这些约束生成以所学抽象为因子的对数线性因子图。ILL不是贝叶斯网络，其格边编码的是抽象的精化与粗化，而非条件依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立ILL与概率图模型、最大熵模型之间的形式化联系，阐明ILL学习规则的概率语义，为推理、可辨识性和混合符号概率学习开辟新方向。

Method: 将ILL的分区过程解释为确定性商变量的诱导，将规则解释为商变量的边际分布；将一般提升解释为满足边际约束的联合分布可行族，将特殊提升（L2均匀性）与最大熵原理建立联系；在Shannon熵提升下导出对数线性因子图表示。

Result: ILL学习的规则集等价于一系列边际约束；一般提升对应满足这些约束的全部联合分布；特殊提升（L2均匀性）等价于最大熵重建；Shannon熵提升下约束生成对数线性因子图，因子由所学抽象索引；ILL的格结构编码抽象的精化/粗化关系而非条件依赖，因此不是贝叶斯网络。

Conclusion: ILL应被视为针对商变量的可解释约束因子图的结构学习框架，这为其与图模型和最大熵模型的关系提供了清晰的理解，并指出了推理、可辨识性和混合符号概率学习的新方向。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [53] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于双参数Weibull框架诊断Transformer权重分布，通过AdamW更新中的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力）解释了权重尺度参数λ(t)在训练中增长、过冲和松弛的动力学机制，并提出了从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力的样条位移方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer权重分布中Weibull尺度参数λ(t)在AdamW训练过程中为何呈现增长、过冲和松弛的动态行为。

Method: 从AdamW更新中推导出权重平方范数的三力分解：对齐力（权重与自适应更新方向的相关性）、注入力（自适应步长幅度）和衰减力（解耦权重衰减）。使用自训练Pythia-70M模型及真实优化器矩进行验证，并引入样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 对齐力主导增长阶段，贡献绝对力预算的88-94%（四组随机种子），且对超权重移除鲁棒。饱和期对齐力与衰减力趋向平衡，解释了增长到松弛的转变。剩余RMS到Weibull重建偏移约5-6%。样条位移方法恢复对齐力准确率约92-94%，约为朴素两基点基线方法的两倍。λ(t)峰值随训练数据相干性变化。

Conclusion: 三力分解有效解释了AdamW训练中Weibull权重尺度参数的动力学行为，样条位移方法为实际场景中优化器矩不可获取时的分析提供了可行方案，数据相干性对权重尺度增长的影响留待后续研究。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [54] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多元零膨胀高斯分布的分布估计算法（ZIG-EDA），首次将EDA推广到稀疏黑箱优化问题，无需手工设计稀疏算子，能联合优化稀疏模式与活跃参数，实验表明在Lunar Lander基准上优于稠密EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EDA在连续参数空间表现优异，但无法直接处理稀疏参数空间（大多数最优系数恰好为零的问题）。已有的稀疏黑箱优化器重新引入了手工设计的稀疏算子、双层优化方案、零阈值等EDA本应避免的偏见性假设，因此需要一种能自然融合稀疏性的EDA方法。

Method: 提出多元零膨胀高斯（ZIG）分布作为EDA的采样分布。该分布通过潜高斯模型分别建模指示维度和值维度，联合表示稀疏模式、活跃参数间相关性及二者交互；从观测样本中可识别潜参数，并引入实用的基于amortized inversion的估计器。

Result: 估计器能准确恢复潜相关结构；在Lunar Lander基准上，ZIG-EDA收敛速度更快、最终回报更高，且找到的控制器仅需少量活跃参数，优于稠密高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA。

Conclusion: ZIG-EDA成功将EDA推广到稀疏黑箱优化，无需手工设计稀疏算子即可联合优化稀疏模式与参数值，为处理大规模稀疏优化问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [55] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPICE方法，仅用30分钟人类数据（比模仿学习少2500倍）作为正则化目标，结合自对弈强化学习训练驾驶策略，在单GPU上15小时完成训练，生成的策略能与未见人类轨迹协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自对弈强化学习训练的驾驶策略会产生与人类不兼容的"外星"驾驶行为，而先前通过奖励工程和域随机化来缓解的方法脆弱且劳动密集。

Method: 在最小安全目标达成奖励基础上，将人类演示数据作为正则化目标（而非完全丢弃），仅使用30分钟人类数据。

Result: 仅需30分钟人类数据（比模仿学习少2500倍），策略能与未见人类轨迹协调，在单消费级GPU上15小时完成训练。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据就能有效引导自对弈强化学习策略向人类兼容方向收敛，大幅降低对人类数据的依赖。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [56] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMUSE是一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，通过基于Dirichlet主观逻辑模型的不确定性量化，自适应决定是否需要引入MRI或PET等昂贵模态，在保持诊断精度的同时减少50-90%的影像数据采集成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期AD诊断依赖多模态数据（临床评估、MRI、PET），但MRI和PET成本高昂且并非普遍可及，导致全模态推理在临床实践中不切实际。如何在保证诊断准确性的前提下降低数据采集成本是核心痛点。

Method: ProMUSE首先使用低成本的临床数据进行证据分类，通过Dirichlet主观逻辑模型量化不确定性；当不确定性超过学习阈值时，逐步引入MRI或PET特征，并利用Dempster-Shafer理论融合各模态的信念和不确定性，从而获得校准的多模态预测。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS三个数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI和MCI-AD任务中，ProMUSE达到与全模态基线相当或更优的准确率，同时将MRI/PET使用量减少50-90%，实现了显著的成本节约。

Conclusion: ProMUSE是一种实用、不确定感知且资源高效的AD筛查解决方案，能够自适应决定何时需要额外模态，在真实临床工作流中平衡诊断准确性与数据采集成本。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

</details>


### [57] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cAPM，一种连续AI辅助起搏标测方法，通过任务无关的代理神经网络、主动学习和持续学习策略，在多个室性心动过速目标间传递知识，将起搏标测所需位点从13.7个减少到4.5个，定位准确率达到81%（5mm容差内），显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室性心动过速是危及生命的心律失常，起搏标测是导管消融的关键步骤，但现有AI方法需对每个目标重新训练，无法跨目标或跨患者迁移知识，导致标测效率仍然有限。

Method: 提出cAPM框架，包含：(1) 任务无关的代理神经网络，学习起搏位点到12导联ECG形态的映射；(2) 主动学习策略，选择信息量最大的起搏位点来优化代理模型；(3) 持续学习策略，顺序学习多个目标同时保留先前知识。可选择性使用经验回放。

Result: 在包含不同生理条件和心室几何形态的序列定位任务测试平台上，cAPM使用4.5个起搏位点实现81%的临床容差内（5mm）定位概率，而现有最优主动学习方法需要13.7个位点，仅达到38%的概率。

Conclusion: cAPM显著提升了起搏标测效率，为后续在体临床前和临床研究提供了坚实基础，有望指导实际起搏标测操作。

Abstract: Ventricular tachycardia is a life-threatening rhythm disorder and a major cause of sudden cardiac death. Pace-mapping is a clinical procedure for identifying the intervention target during catheter ablation of VT. It requires clinicians to pace different sites in the ventricles and rapidly interpret the resulting electrocardiograms to determine where to pace next or whether a target site has been identified. Active learning AI models have been proposed to guide clinicians to the next pacing site, showing promise in reducing the number of pacing sites and improving the efficiency of pace-mapping. Existing methods require retraining each target without the ability to transfer knowledge across multiple VTs within the same patient or across patients. We introduce cAPM for continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping to capture and transfer knowledge accumulated from past pace-mapping data to reduce the number of pace-mapping data needed for future target VTs. This is made possible by a task-agnostic surrogate neural network that learns the mapping from pacing sites to 12-lead ECG morphology, an active-learning strategy that refines this surrogate model by selecting the most informative pacing site for each target, and a continual learning strategy to do so sequentially while retaining knowledge from prior targets. Evaluated on an in-silico testbed consisting of sequentially-presented localization tasks across different physiological conditions and ventricular geometries, cAPM with and without replay of past data samples achieved an 81% probability of localizing within clinical tolerance (5 mm accuracy) using 4.5 pace-mapping sites, compared to the state-of-the-art active-learning method achieving 38% probability using 13.7 pacing sites. These results provide a strong basis for preparing cAPM towards in-vivo preclinical and clinical studies where it can be used to guide pace-mapping.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的蛋白质建模方法主要依赖序列邻接或几何邻近性，未能充分反映蛋白质折叠的物理原理（如二级结构元件和氢键相互作用），因此需要一种能更好地编码蛋白质结构归纳偏置的图表示学习方法。

Method: 1) 残基级节点表示融合二级结构分配信息；2) 基于氢键相互作用构建图边，并通过能量强度进行过滤；3) 使用图神经网络学习蛋白质表示。

Result: 在常用蛋白质基准任务上，该方法相比现有图方法取得了一致的性能提升，且学习到的图连通性与已知结构基序对齐，增强了生物学可解释性。

Conclusion: 引入二级结构和能量过滤的氢键拓扑结构为蛋白质表示学习提供了有效的归纳偏置，该方法有助于更准确和可解释的蛋白质建模。

Abstract: Graph-based representations are widely used in protein modeling, yet many existing approaches rely primarily on sequence adjacency or geometric proximity, which only partially reflect the principles governing protein folding. Proteins instead adopt complex three-dimensional conformations organized around secondary structure elements, such as $α$-helices and $β$-sheets, which encode recurring local motifs and stabilizing hydrogen-bond interactions. In this work, we introduce a secondary-structure-aware graph neural network for protein representation learning. Residue-level node representations are augmented with secondary structure assignments, and graph edges are constructed from hydrogen-bond interactions filtered by their energetic strength. This design enables the model to capture both local structural context and long-range couplings that are central to protein stability and function. We evaluate the proposed approach on commonly used protein benchmarks and observe consistent improvements over existing graph-based methods. In addition, the resulting graph representations offer enhanced biological interpretability, as the learned connectivity aligns with established structural motifs. These findings suggest that incorporating secondary structure and energy-filtered hydrogen-bond topology provides an effective inductive bias for protein representation learning. The code is released at https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet

</details>


### [59] [Physics-Informed Discovery of Yield Functions in Plasticity via Convex Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19375)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Donghyuk Cho,Jecheon Yu,Jeong Whan Yoon,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息驱动的框架，能够仅从全场位移和反力数据中识别各向异性屈服函数，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量或预设参数化形式，并通过凸神经网络表示屈服函数，结合可微应力更新和物理信息力平衡损失进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性屈服函数的识别面临三大挑战：屈服现象无法直接观测、方向性校准需要大量加载方向、选择合适的解析形式非常困难。现有方法依赖直接应力空间监督，缺乏从全场力学测量数据中识别屈服函数的有效途径。

Method: 采用凸神经网络表示屈服函数，强制满足凸性和一次正齐次性及拉压对称性；将屈服函数作为弹塑性应力积分中的力学约束成分，通过可微分的应力更新算法和多个加载工况下的物理信息力平衡损失函数进行训练，无需应力标签或屈服面数据。

Result: 通过von Mises、Hill 1948和Yld2000-2d屈服函数的有限元基准验证，评估了屈服轮廓一致性、位移噪声敏感性、塑性活跃应力状态的可识别性、认知不确定性和多项式代理部署效果。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条力学约束的路径，能够从位移和力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，并将识别出的成分保持在弹塑性应力积分的结构体系内。

Abstract: Identifying anisotropic yield functions remains challenging since yielding is not directly observed in full-field mechanical measurements, directional calibration can require many loading directions, and selecting an appropriate analytical form is nontrivial. This study proposes a physics-informed framework for discovering yield functions from full-field displacement data and reaction force data, without stress observations, plastic strain measurements, direct yield surface data, or a prescribed parametric yield function. The framework identifies the yield function as a mechanically constrained constitutive component inside elastoplastic stress integration, rather than through direct stress-space supervision. The yield function is represented by a convex neural network that enforces convexity and positive homogeneity of degree one while imposing the assumed tension-compression symmetry, and this neural yield function is trained with a differentiable stress update and a physics-informed force equilibrium loss across multiple loading cases. The proposed framework is validated using finite element (FE) benchmark studies with von Mises, Hill 1948, and Yld2000-2d yield functions, assessing yield contour agreement, displacement-noise sensitivity, identifiability through plastically active stress states, epistemic uncertainty, and polynomial-surrogate deployment. This study provides a mechanics-constrained pathway for discovering anisotropic yield functions from displacement and force data while keeping the identified component within the structure of elastoplastic stress integration.

</details>


### [60] [Emyx: Fast and efficient all-atom protein generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19377)
*Nicholas J. Williams,Ward Haddadin,Matteo P. Ferla,Constantin Schneider,Nicholas B. Woodall,Ruby Sedgwick,Christian D. Madsen,Andrew L. Hopkins,Edward O. Pyzer-Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Emyx是一个140M参数的等变流匹配模型，通过轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接替代传统复杂嵌入，并推导了流匹配与EDM噪声框架的精确重参数化，在仅需682 GPU小时（约RFdiffusion3的1/4）训练时间下，于AME酶设计基准中全面超越Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3，包括成功率、结构新颖性、多样性和几何有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全原子生成模型继承了结构预测中的昂贵架构，导致训练成本高且样本多样性有限。作者认为生成模型并不需要复杂的共进化信号，而应专注于稀疏几何约束，因此旨在设计更轻量、高效的酶设计生成模型。

Method: Emyx采用140M参数的conditional flow matching模型，使用标准transformer模块替代重嵌入堆叠，引入轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接；同时推导了流匹配插值与EDM噪声水平框架的精确重参数化，将流匹配训练效率与扩散模型先进采样方法桥接。

Result: Emyx在AME酶设计基准上，在成功率（要求全局折叠恢复和催化几何精度）、结构新颖性、支架多样性和几何有效性方面均优于Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3，训练仅需682 GPU小时，约为RFdiffusion3的1/4。

Conclusion: Emyx证明了在计算酶设计中，轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接可以替代复杂架构实现SOTA性能，同时大幅降低训练成本；流匹配与EDM框架的桥接为生成模型提供了更高效的训练和采样策略。

Abstract: Computational enzyme design requires generating proteins that scaffold catalytic residues and ligands, a task that demands both geometric accuracy and structural diversity from the underlying generative model. Current all-atom generators inherit expensive architectures from structure prediction, leading to high training costs and limited sample diversity. We argue that much of this complexity is unnecessary for generators, which condition on sparse geometric constraints rather than rich co-evolutionary signals. Emyx is a 140M-parameter conditional flow matching model that concentrates capacity within standard transformer blocks, replacing heavy embedding stacks with lightweight conditional representations and sparse connectivity. We additionally derive an exact reparametrisation of the flow matching interpolant into the EDM noise-level framework, bridging flow matching training efficiency with state-of-the-art sampling methods designed for diffusion models without retraining. Despite being the smallest model, Emyx outperforms both Proteína-Complexa and RFdiffusion3 against the AME enzyme design benchmark across success rate under strict evaluation requiring both global fold recovery and catalytic geometry accuracy, structural novelty, scaffold diversity, and geometric validity, while training in just $682$ GPU-hours, roughly $4\times$ less than RFdiffusion3.

</details>


### [61] [A Hybrid GNN-FEM Framework for Phase-Field Fracture Simulation. Physics-Preserving Hybridization for Generalizable Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19378)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Yongjin Choi,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合GNN-FEM框架用于相场断裂模拟，用图神经网络替代相场更新步骤，保留FEM位移求解器，在保持精度的同时显著降低计算成本，并在几何、载荷、材料属性和离散化方面展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场断裂模拟虽提供鲁棒的变分框架，但求解耦合、非线性、历史依赖系统的增量有限元过程计算成本极高，限制了实际应用。

Method: 将图神经网络代理集成到传统交错求解方案中，替代每个载荷增量步的相场更新，保留基于FEM的位移求解器以确保力学平衡和边界条件；采用无量纲特征设计、基于网格的图表示和由控制相场方程导出的物理信息损失函数。

Result: 混合方法相比传统FEM降低了计算成本，同时保持准确性；在不同几何形状、载荷条件、材料属性和离散化下展现出鲁棒的预测性能。

Conclusion: 选择性代理策略强调识别物理上有意义且增量结构化的学习目标，而非暴力数据生成来学习完整断裂过程，为历史依赖的非线性物理问题提供了高效且可泛化的求解范式。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning (SciML) has emerged as a promising approach for accelerating simulations of complex physical systems, yet achieving physically consistent and generalizable predictions for nonlinear, history-dependent problems remains a central challenge. In this study, we propose a hybrid GNN--FEM framework for efficient and generalizable phase-field fracture modeling. While phase-field approaches provide a robust variational framework for simulating complex crack evolution, their high computational cost limits practical applications because they require solving coupled, nonlinear, and history-dependent systems within an incremental finite element procedure. To address this challenge, a graph neural network surrogate is integrated into the conventional staggered scheme, replacing the phase-field update at each load increment while retaining the FEM-based displacement solver to enforce mechanical equilibrium and boundary conditions. By preserving the incremental solution structure, the framework remains consistent with history-dependent fracture evolution without requiring the surrogate to approximate the full solution trajectory. This selective surrogate strategy emphasizes the identification of a physically meaningful and incrementally structured learning target, rather than relying on brute-force data generation to learn the full fracture process. The proposed framework achieves strong generalization across varying geometries, loading conditions, material properties, and discretizations through dimensionless feature design, a graph-based formulation on mesh-based domains, and a physics-informed loss derived from the governing phase-field equation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the hybrid approach reduces computational cost while maintaining accuracy compared with conventional FEM, and exhibits robust predictive performance across diverse problem settings.

</details>


### [62] [How Linear Is a Transformer Feed-Forward Block? Per-Block Linear Recoverability Is Learned, Not Architectural](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19379)
*Stuart Whipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线性可恢复性（R²_lin）指标，通过最小二乘线性近似分析Transformer FFN层的非线性程度。在GPT-2、Pythia-160m和llama-160m中，FFN的线性度在块间高度异质且非单调（R²范围从>0.99到<0.3），由训练习得而非架构决定。低秩双线性探针仅能恢复少量残差，未恢复部分呈高阶/分布式结构。该指标可作为压缩信号：高可恢复性块可用8倍参数压缩替换（性能损失+0.77 perplexity），低可恢复性块则不可行。同时揭示了线性基线欠收敛的方法论问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer FFN通常被视为非线性计算单元，但训练后的FFN块实际非线性程度从未被精确量化测量，缺乏系统评估其线性可恢复性的标准方法。

Method: 将每个FFN视为逐位置的输入-输出映射，分解为精确最小二乘线性近似与残差，计算闭式线性映射解释的留出方差比例作为线性可恢复性（R²_lin）；使用低秩双线性探针分析残差结构；基于R²_lin指标进行压缩实验验证其应用价值。

Result: GPT-2、Pythia-160m和llama-160m的12个FFN块中R²_lin高度异质且非单调（相邻块间从>0.99到<0.3）；同宽度不同模型（GPT-2与Pythia-160m）分布截然不同；低秩双线性探针仅恢复少量残差；高可恢复性块可用8倍参数压缩（+0.77 perplexity）。

Conclusion: FFN的非线性是训练习得的块级属性而非架构固有性质；线性可恢复性可作为有效的压缩信号指导选择性替换；传统线性基线存在欠收敛问题，需采用闭式最小二乘解作为评估基准。

Abstract: Transformer feed-forward networks (FFNs) are often treated as nonlinear stores of computation, yet how nonlinear a trained FFN block actually is has rarely been measured. We treat each FFN as a position-wise input-to-output map and split it into the exact least-squares linear approximation plus a residual. The held-out variance the closed-form linear map explains defines a block's linear recoverability (R^2_lin), an optimiser-free measure of its linearity.
  Across all twelve blocks of GPT-2, Pythia-160m, and llama-160m, R^2_lin is highly heterogeneous and non-monotone with depth, ranging from near-linear (>0.99) to strongly nonlinear (<0.3) between adjacent blocks, and is not set by the activation function: same-width GELU models GPT-2 and Pythia-160m have sharply different profiles, so recoverability is a learned property of individual trained blocks, not an architectural one. A low-rank bilinear probe of the residual recovers only a few points of R^2, with gain uncorrelated with residual nonlinearity: the unrecovered computation is not a single position-wise product but higher-order or distributed structure.
  The measurement also serves as a targeted compression signal: recoverable blocks admit large single-layer replacements (GPT-2's early FFN at 8x fewer parameters for +0.77 perplexity), while low-recoverability blocks flag where this is unsafe. It further exposes a methodological pitfall: trained linear baselines can badly under-converge on ill-conditioned transformer activations, so we report the exact closed-form least-squares ceiling throughout.

</details>


### [63] [VERITAS: Verifier-Guided Proof Search for Zero-Shot Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19399)
*Manish Acharya,Zhenyu Liao,Yueke Zhang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERITAS是一个零样本框架，通过两阶段协议（Best-of-N采样+批评引导的MCTS）将验证器信号（语法错误、类型不匹配等）反馈到证明搜索中，在miniF2F上达到40.6%，并发布了55个定理的组合学基准VERITAS-CombiBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的形式化证明器常将丰富的验证器信号（语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）简化为二元通过/失败信号，丢失了宝贵信息。

Method: 两阶段协议：Phase 1进行Best-of-N采样；Phase 2使用批评引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），将Phase 1的失败作为显式负例输入，并通过协议设计保证Phase 2的额外求解可归因于反馈驱动的探索。

Result: miniF2F上达到40.6%（Best-of-5为36.9%，Portfolio为26.2%）；VERITAS-CombiBench上达到7.3%（Best-of-5为1.8%，Portfolio为3.6%），证明无引导采样在需要从验证器反馈中迭代恢复正确引理名时会损害性能。

Conclusion: VERITAS通过将非二元验证器信号反馈回搜索过程显著提升了形式化证明性能，并暴露了无引导采样在特定场景下的局限性。

Abstract: LLM-based formal provers often collapse rich verifier signals (syntax errors, type mismatches, partial goal progress) into a binary pass/fail bit. We present VERITAS, a zero-shot framework that routes every verifier signal back into proof search through a two-phase protocol: Best-of-N sampling first, then a critic-guided MCTS pass that ingests Phase 1 failures as explicit negative examples. The protocol preserves every theorem solved by its own Phase 1 sweep, so Phase 2's additional solves are attributable to feedback-driven exploration. VERITAS reaches 40.6% on miniF2F (vs. an independently run Best-of-5 at 36.9%, Portfolio 26.2%) and 7.3% on VERITAS-CombiBench, a 55-theorem combinatorics benchmark we release on which Best-of-5 (1.8%) falls below Portfolio (3.6%), exposing that unguided sampling hurts when correct lemma names must be recovered iteratively from verifier feedback. Artifacts are available on GitHub.

</details>


### [64] [Thermodynamic Signatures of Reasoning: Free-Energy and Spectral-Form-Factor Diagnostics for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19404)
*Salim Khazem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自由能签名（Fes）作为注意力图谱拉普拉斯算子的谱描述符，通过热力学势和随机矩阵理论统计量检测LLM幻觉，在六个开源模型和六个基准上取得最优AUROC，无需更新模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力图谱拉普拉斯谱的幻觉检测方法仅使用少量特征值或手工标量，忽视了谱结构中的大部分信息。

Method: 将注意力拉普拉斯矩阵视为哈密顿量，提取配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容等热力学势，结合随机矩阵理论的谱形状因子（SFF）；理论证明Lipschitz稳定性、表达性及PAC上界；基于RMT偏差的无监督得分。

Result: 六个开源LLM和六个基准上，轻量级探针平均AUROC超越LapEig +6.5、GoR-4 +2.4；无监督RMT偏差得分平均AUROC 0.71；正确生成呈现Wigner-Dyson谱统计，幻觉呈现Poisson谱统计。

Conclusion: Fes作为更丰富的谱描述符能显著提升幻觉检测性能，且无需修改底层LLM；RMT分析揭示了正确与错误生成在谱统计上的可区分模式。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) is deployment-critical, and recent work shows that the spectrum of attention-derived graph Laplacians carries strong signal about reasoning quality. Prior spectral diagnostics, however, summarize the Laplacian spectrum by a handful of eigenvalues or hand-picked scalars, leaving most of its structure unused. We propose Free-Energy Signatures (Fes), a spectral descriptor that treats each layer's attention Laplacian as a Hamiltonian and extracts its thermodynamic potentials partition function, free energy, spectral entropy, heat capacity together with the random-matrix-theory (RMT) spectral form factor. We prove three results: (i)~Lipschitz stability of Fes under attention perturbation; (ii)~an expressiveness result showing that Fes enriches finite spectral summaries and approximates moment-derived spectral functionals under explicit regularity and grid-resolution assumptions; and (iii)~a finite-sample PAC bound on the AUROC of a training-free detector built from Fes. Empirically, across six open-weight LLMs and six benchmarks, a lightweight probe on Fes descriptors achieves the strongest aggregate AUROC among attention-spectral baselines, improving over LapEig by $+6.5$ AUROC points and over GoR-4 by $+2.4$ points on average, while requiring no update to the underlying LLM. In the fully unsupervised setting, an RMT-deviation score achieves mean AUROC $0.71$, providing a label-free but weaker detector. A complementary RMT analysis shows that correct generations exhibit more Wigner-Dyson like spectral statistics, whereas hallucinations exhibit more Poisson-like statistics. The anonymized code and config are provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [65] [FlexLAM: Resolving the Bottleneck Trade-off in Latent Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19408)
*Takanori Yoshimoto,Yang Hu,Naruya Kondo,Tatsuya Matsushima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlexLAM提出变长潜变量动作，通过嵌套丢弃训练替代固定容量瓶颈，在不改变架构的情况下实现从紧凑到详细的可变编码，在多个预算下匹配或超越固定容量LAM，支持推理时调整且无需重新训练，是固定容量瓶颈的即插即用升级方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜变量动作模型（LAMs）存在固定容量瓶颈的权衡问题：编码过紧会丢失动作对齐所需的过渡线索，编码过松则保留多余变异，在标注稀缺或分布狭窄时难以分辨。

Method: 用嵌套丢弃（nested dropout）训练变长潜变量动作，生成前缀有效（prefix-valid）的编码——先捕获紧凑过渡结构，仅在需要时增加细节，无需新架构或损失函数。

Result: 单一FlexLAM在每个评估的token预算下匹配或超越独立训练的固定容量LAM；在标准稀缺标签监督和低回报单任务对齐压力测试中表现更优；支持推理时调整token预算无需重训；改进了Ego4D过渡重建。

Conclusion: 变长潜变量动作是固定容量瓶颈的架构无关、即插即用升级方案，可应用于潜变量动作模型、潜变量世界模型和视频预训练动作接口。

Abstract: Latent actions provide a compact interface between action-free video and downstream decision-making, yet existing Latent Action Models (LAMs) force every transition through a fixed-capacity bottleneck. We identify a bottleneck trade-off: overly tight codes can discard transition cues needed for action alignment, while overly loose codes preserve additional transition variation that must be resolved when alignment labels are scarce or narrowly distributed. FlexLAM replaces this fixed capacity with variable-length latent actions trained by nested dropout, yielding prefix-valid codes that capture compact transition structure first and add detail only when needed, without new architectures or losses. A single FlexLAM matches or surpasses separately trained fixed-capacity LAMs at every evaluated token budget under standard scarce-label supervision and under a low-return single-task alignment stress test, indicating that FlexLAM is not merely adjustable at inference time but learns a better latent-action interface at the same token budgets. The same model supports inference-time token-budget adjustment without retraining, and FlexLAM improves Ego4D transition reconstruction. These results suggest that variable-length latent actions are an architecture-free, drop-in upgrade to the fixed-capacity bottleneck in latent action models, latent-action world models, and video-pretrained action interfaces.

</details>


### [66] [Spectral DPPs via NEPv: A Scalable Continuous Relaxation of Determinantal MAP for Diversity-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19411)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将DPP-MAP（行列式点过程的最大后验子集选择）问题重新转化为Stiefel流形上的连续优化，并发现其最优性条件构成一种新型的非线性特征值问题（NEPv）。作者设计了基于自洽场（SCF）迭代的求解器，利用谱间隙保证局部收敛，算法仅需矩阵-向量乘积，复杂度为O((ndk+nk²)t)，在n上呈近线性缩放，适用于百万到十亿级别的大规模数据选择场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPP的MAP子集选择在数据策展、主动学习、上下文示例选择等现代机器学习任务中至关重要，但其NP-hard性质和现有贪心/采样算法在n上超线性缩放的高昂计算成本，限制了在大规模（百万至十亿级）数据场景下的实际应用。作者旨在提出一种理论上可扩展、能利用谱间隙保证收敛的连续优化求解方法。

Method: 将离散的DPP-MAP问题（最大化logdet(L_S)）重新表述为Stiefel流形上的连续优化问题；推导其一阶最优性条件，发现其构成一种新型的、特征向量依赖于算子的非线性特征值问题（NEPv）；设计自洽场（SCF）迭代求解该NEPv，利用谱间隙给出局部收缩收敛保证；算法仅需与核矩阵的矩阵-向量乘积，复杂度为O((ndk+nk²)t)（t为迭代次数，通常很小），可结合低秩和特征映射核。

Result: 提出了名为DPP-SCF的算法，理论上具有局部收敛保证（基于谱间隙），复杂度近线性于n（O((ndk+nk²)t)），避免了现有方法在n上的超线性开销；该算法可直接利用低秩和特征映射核，适用于现代机器学习中的大规模数据场景（百万至十亿级候选集）。

Conclusion: 本文从理论层面完成了问题松弛、求解器设计和复杂度分析，证明DPP-MAP可转化为具有局部收敛保证的可扩展连续优化问题。全面的实际数据基准测试留待后续实证研究，但本文为大规模高多样性子集选择提供了一种新的理论可行方案。

Abstract: Selecting a small, diverse, high-quality subset from a massive pool of candidates is a recurring primitive in modern machine learning -- data curation and coreset selection for training and fine-tuning large models, active-learning batch acquisition, prompt and exemplar selection for in-context learning, retrieval diversification, and experimental design. Determinantal Point Processes (\DPP s) give a principled, well-calibrated notion of diversity for this task, but their \emph{MAP} objective -- pick a size-$k$ subset $S$ maximizing $\logdet(L_S)$ -- is NP-hard, and the standard greedy and sampling algorithms scale superlinearly in the ground-set size $n$. This cost is prohibitive precisely in the data-centric regime where diversity matters most, where $n$ ranges over millions to billions of candidate examples, features, or embeddings. We recast \DPP-MAP as a continuous optimization problem over the Stiefel manifold, and show that its first-order optimality conditions form a \emph{Nonlinear Eigenvalue Problem with eigenvector dependency} (\NEPv) of a previously unstudied form. This \NEPv\ admits a self-consistent field (\SCF) iteration with a spectral-gap-based local contraction guarantee, giving a principled iterative solver where the diversity objective drives an eigenvector-dependent operator. The resulting algorithm, \OurMethod, requires only matrix-vector products with the kernel and runs in time $O\!\big((ndk+nk^2)\,t\big)$ for a small number of iterations $t$, scaling near-linearly in $n$ and integrating directly with low-rank and feature-map kernels common in ML. This paper focuses on the relaxation, solver, and scaling analysis; full real-data benchmarking is left to a planned empirical study.

</details>


### [67] [Spectral Retrieval-Augmented Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19412)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecReTF方法，通过将时间序列转换为窗口化频率表示、使用结合振幅和相位信息的联合相似度度量，以及应用指数移动平均加权方案来平衡近期与历史信息，解决了现有检索增强时间序列预测方法中的频谱盲区和时间近因两大局限性，在多个基准数据集上取得了优于时域检索方法的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列预测方法在处理复杂非平稳模式时面临困难，检索增强方法虽被提出但存在两个根本性局限：频谱盲区（忽视频域周期结构特征）和时间近因（对历史数据平等对待，未强调近期相关模式）。

Method: SpecReTF方法包括三个关键步骤：(1) 将时间序列转换为窗口化频率表示；(2) 使用结合振幅和相位信息的联合度量计算相似度；(3) 应用指数移动平均（EMA）加权方案，在检索时平衡近期与历史上下文。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，SpecReTF在预测精度上优于传统的时域检索方法，能够有效处理多样化的非平稳时间序列。

Conclusion: 通过引入频域信息捕获周期性结构并结合EMA加权平衡时间近因，SpecReTF有效克服了现有检索增强预测方法的两个核心缺陷，为非平稳时间序列预测提供了更优的解决方案。
